# Remix Icon (`ri`) — 3058 icons

- **Prefix:** `Ri` · **Version:** 4.6.0 · **License:** Apache-2.0 · **Style:** fill · **viewBox:** `0 0 24 24`
- **Source:** [Remix-Design/RemixIcon](https://github.com/Remix-Design/RemixIcon)
- Neutral, open-source 24×24 system icons in matching `-line` and `-fill` pairs (e.g. `RiHome2Line`, `RiHome2Fill`, `RiSearchLine`). Single-color fill; theme via `color`.

Import default-only per icon: `import RiExample from "@mal-icons/react/ri/RiExample"`, or named from the set barrel: `import { RiExample } from "@mal-icons/react/ri"`.
